<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：长时域人形机器人全身操作（long-horizon humanoid loco-manipulation）；场景：多目标重排（multi-object rearrangement）；传感器类型：摘要未说明；目标类别：机器人操作对象，非遥感目标。

Model architecture: 分层模型：底层策略（low-level policy）通过对抗模仿学习训练，高层策略（high-level policy）与宏动力学世界模型（macro-dynamics world model）联合学习。世界模型预测潜在决策引起的时域扩展状态转移。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。论文未讨论任何模型压缩、加速或轻量化方法。

Onboard deployability: 不涉及。论文未讨论参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗或星上/边缘部署。

Datasets and metrics: 模拟环境中的多目标重排场景；评价指标：全任务成功率（full-task success）和部分完成率（partial-completion rates）。

Experiments: 在多种模拟多目标重排场景下，LUCID相比基线方法在全任务成功率和部分完成率上均有提升。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关，无直接可参考的局限性信息。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，基本无可借鉴点。该论文的分层模型强化学习思想（高层规划+底层技能）在机器人领域有意义，但与遥感在轨目标检测模型轻量化（如剪枝、量化、蒸馏、轻量网络设计）无直接关联。

Abstract: Long-horizon humanoid loco-manipulation requires composing versatile whole-body skills and reliable high-level decision making. Existing methods often coordinate pretrained skills with scripted planners, finite-state machines or task-specific model-free policies, restricting their ability to handle complex task sequences. To address this limitation, we propose \textbf{LUCID}, a hierarchical model-based reinforcement learning framework that plans over reusable skills through imagined rollouts of a learned dynamics model. LUCID first trains a structured latent-conditioned low-level policy via adversarial imitation and then freezes it while jointly learning a high-level policy and macro-dynamics world model. The world model predicts the temporally extended state transitions induced by latent decisions, enabling high-level policy optimization through imagined rollouts. We evaluate our framework across various simulated multi-object rearrangement scenarios. Experimental results show that LUCID improves the full-task success and partial-completion rates compared to prior baseline methods, demonstrating its effectiveness in complex sequential loco-manipulation tasks.

</details>


### [92] [From Benchmark Performance to Tool Deployment: Human-in-the-Loop Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07770)
*Mike Szklarzewski,CJ George,Gavin Smithson,Christopher Stokes,Dakota Fulp,William M. Jones,Benjamin Wynn,Alexander Ur,Agit Yesiloz,Clint Kallenbach,Mark Swartz,Nathan DeBardeleben,Sharmistha Chakrabarti*

Abstract: Automated anomaly detection methods often report strong performance on curated academic benchmarks, but their behavior under real-world industrial conditions is less clear. In this work, we evaluate 19 unsupervised anomaly detection models on the BowTie dataset, a challenging manufacturing dataset with reflective surfaces, subtle defects, and profile-specific variation. In contrast to benchmark results, we observe that model performance is less stable than typically reported on standard benchmarks such as MVTec AD, highly sensitive to preprocessing, and inconsistent across conditions, with no single approach emerging as uniformly robust; a consensus audit further indicates that nominal-data quality affects deployment.
  Motivated by these findings, we developed and initially deployed a unified human-in-the-loop framework for manufactured-part inspection that combines image annotation, AI-assisted defect detection, and an integrated validation engine, replacing a prior manual visual inspection and documentation workflow. The system supports heatmap-guided defect review, SAM-refined candidate regions for inspector acceptance, rejection, or boundary adjustment, mask evaluation where annotations exist, and review history for inspector consistency and onboarding. Together, the results highlight the gap between benchmark performance and deployment reality, and provide a practical framework for addressing it.

</details>

Abstract: We study policy-based reinforcement learning under the $μ$-resets interaction protocol of Kakade and Langford [KL02]. This interaction protocol enables the learner to sample trajectories from a given exploratory reset distribution $μ$, in addition to the starting distribution. We resolve the question raised by [KLS25] on the role of policy realizability for the sample complexity of this problem. Critically, the dependence on horizon $H$ is governed by the notion of coverage assumed of the reset distribution. Under bounded all-policy concentrability, we show a $\exp(Ω(H))$ sample complexity lower bound; with bounded pushforward concentrability, we show the dependence on horizon is tightly characterized as $\exp(Θ(\sqrt H))$.

</details>

*Inesh Chakrabarti,Sourjya Roy,Bowen Bao,Thiago Crepaldi,Spandan Tiwari,Ashish Sirasao*

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) language models deliver high capacity at low per-token compute, but deploying them cheaply requires compressing their many expert weight matrices. Expert pruning (e.g., REAP) and merging reduce cost but sacrifice accuracy and require retraining the router; low-rank delta decomposition of experts (e.g., D^2-MoE) preserves all experts and the router, but degrades sharply as the expert count grows because a single shared component cannot approximate many near-orthogonal experts.
  Because MoE expert weights are near-orthogonal, a single shared component (as in prior delta decomposition) scales poorly with the expert count; we show that experts nonetheless organize into functional co-activation communities that are decoupled from weight similarity. Building on this, we introduce LorExperts, a router-preserving compression method that clusters experts, keeps one full-precision dominant per cluster, and represents the remaining members as low-rank corrections to their local dominant. LorExperts retains all experts and the original router (no router retraining). At ~50% expert compression on Qwen3-30B-A3B and Gemma-4-26B-A4B, LorExperts preserves downstream accuracy and perplexity better than the baselines on most of the tasks; the margin over D^2-MoE grows with expert count E. We further give a reconstruction fine-tuning procedure for LorExperts, and BTExperts, a tree organization of dominants and corrections that enables inference-time amortization of shared computation.

</details>

Ideas for my research: 借鉴意义有限但存在间接启发：(1) 置信度校准思想可迁移到遥感目标检测的置信度校准，例如检测框置信度与真实精度对齐；(2) Monte Carlo Dropout作为一种不确定性估计方法，可用于遥感检测模型的不确定性量化；(3) 事后校准方法（Platt scaling、isotonic regression）可应用于检测模型输出概率校准。但整体与"在轨遥感目标检测模型轻量化"核心方向关联较弱。

Abstract: Confidence estimation for large language models (LLMs) aims to estimate the probability that a generated answer is correct, while calibration aligns these estimates with empirical accuracy. Prior work has shown that token probabilities are often overconfident, we investigate whether these readily available signals can nevertheless provide well-calibrated confidence estimation for mathematical question answering. We compare single-pass estimators, which reuse token probabilities from the original generation, with multi-pass estimators, which obtain additional confidence signals through verification or stochastic forward passes. While individual token probabilities can be highly saturated, we find that aggregating token probabilities over the full sequence captures small but consistent differences between correct and incorrect generations, yielding more informative confidence estimates. Multi-pass methods can yield calibrated confidence estimates. We study two such approaches: self-verification through re-prompting, including a lower-cost in-situ variant, and Monte Carlo Dropout, which derives confidence from variation across stochastic forward passes. We further evaluate two post-hoc calibration methods, Platt scaling and isotonic regression, both of which substantially reduce in-domain calibration error. However, their data efficiency varies with dataset difficulty, and the calibration mappings often transfer asymmetrically across datasets and models.

</details>


### [96] [TEMPER: Tensorized Efficient Manifold-constrained Parameterization for Expressive Residual Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07851)
*Yuxuan Gu,Wuyang Zhou,Huijun Xing,Danilo Mandic*

TL;DR: 提出 TEMPER，用张量网络对超连接（Hyper-connections）中的生成器进行结构化低秩参数化，在保持流形约束路由接口的同时大幅减少额外参数，在语言建模和常识推理任务上以更少参数达到或超越现有方法。

Model architecture: 基于 Hyper-connections（HC）与 manifold-constrained HC（mHC）架构，提出 TEMPER：将 pre-branch aggregation、residual mixing、post-branch redistribution 三个生成器表示为 input-stream、feature、output-stream 三个模态上的多路张量，并用 tensor networks（张量网络）进行结构化低秩参数化。张量秩控制学习到的路由子空间维度，满秩可恢复稠密路由。

Experiments: 在语言建模和常识推理任务上，TEMPER 匹配或超越现有方法，同时使用更少的额外参数。在 8 个 residual streams 时，TEMPER 取得最佳 CORE score，同时比 mHC 少约 84% 的额外参数，展现出更强的性能-参数效率权衡。

Limitations: 摘要未说明。主要局限：1) 面向语言建模/常识推理，未涉及视觉或遥感任务；2) 未提供绝对参数量、FLOPs、推理延迟等部署指标；3) 未说明张量网络低秩分解在硬件（如 FPGA/NPU）上的实际加速效果；4) 未讨论对目标检测等视觉任务的适用性。

Ideas for my research: 1) 张量网络低秩参数化思想可迁移到遥感目标检测骨干网络或检测头中，用于压缩冗余参数；2) 将 residual routing 的"多流动态信息流"思想用于轻量化检测网络的特征融合，在保持表达能力的同时降低参数；3) 张量秩作为可解释的超参数，可借鉴用于控制轻量化模型的压缩程度与精度权衡；4) 但需注意该方法面向序列/语言任务，迁移到 CNN/Transformer 视觉检测需重新设计张量模态，且需补充 FLOPs、延迟、能耗等部署指标验证。

Abstract: Residual connections rely on a static residual pathway, and are essential for training deep neural networks. Hyper-connections (HC) increase the expressivity of residual routing by incorporating multiple residual streams and learning dynamic information flow, while manifold-constrained (mHC) variants stabilize training through doubly stochastic residual mixing. However, a generator-level bottleneck remains in existing methods: they use dense, unstructured generators for pre-branch aggregation, residual mixing, and post-branch redistribution, which results in parameter count growing rapidly with the number of streams. To address this issue, we propose \underline{\textbf{T}}ensorized \underline{\textbf{E}}fficient \underline{\textbf{M}}anifold-constrained \underline{\textbf{P}}arameterization for \underline{\textbf{E}}xpressive Residual \underline{\textbf{R}}outing (\textbf{TEMPER}), which represents these generators as multi-way tensors over the input-stream, feature, and output-stream modes, and parameterizes them using tensor networks. Such a structured low-rank formulation is shown to preserve token-dependent manifold-constrained routing interface while substantially reducing parameter growth. It also promotes interpretability and intuition, as: i) tensor ranks control the dimensionality of the learned routing subspace, with full ranks recovering dense routing; while ii) the generator approximation errors bound differences in routing logits and, consequently, in the routed-block outputs. Comprehensive experiments show that TEMPER matches or outperforms existing methods across language modeling and commonsense reasoning tasks, while requiring substantially fewer additional parameters. At eight residual streams, TEMPER achieves the best CORE score while using about $84\%$ fewer additional parameters than mHC, thus showing a stronger performance-parameter efficiency trade-off.

</details>


### [97] [CommitKV: Lifecycle-Aware KV Cache Compression via Commit Transitions for Multi-Turn Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07855)
*Weizhong Huang,Jinchao Zhang,Xiawu Zheng*

  <summary>Details</summary>

Limitations: 1. 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测完全无关；2. 仅针对LLM Agent场景，不适用于视觉模型；3. 未提供具体性能指标（压缩率、速度提升倍数等）；4. 方法依赖"commit transitions"这一Agent特有机制，难以迁移到遥感目标检测场景。

Abstract: Multi-turn Reasoning-and-Acting (ReAct) agents accumulate growing trajectories of reasoning, tool calls, and observations. Their key-value (KV) caches grow accordingly, increasing memory use and attention cost during model inference. Existing KV cache compression methods reduce these costs by evicting states with low attention scores. However, low attention in the current turn does not imply future irrelevance, as temporarily inactive information may become important later. Snapshot-based eviction methods therefore do not explicitly distinguish temporarily dormant information from information that appears to have completed its role. In this paper, we present CommitKV, which identifies KV lifecycles through commit transitions. Specifically, CommitKV first divides completed agent events into token pages and compares each eligible page's deletion effect before a tool-call commit and after the commit's returned observation has been incorporated. Based on these paired measurements, CommitKV distinguishes dormant pages from high-to-low completion candidates. It then applies a greedy joint test, accepting candidates for retirement only when their combined post-commit effect remains bounded. Finally, at a later compression checkpoint, accepted pages are excluded, a bounded set of pages awaiting post-commit measurement is protected, and the remaining KV states are retained within the cache budget using the same token indices for keys, values, and absolute positions. These mechanisms ensure that CommitKV can distinguish dormant information from information that has completed its observed role and can be safely removed. Experiments on various benchmarks show that CommitKV reduces agent memory use, accelerates end-to-end inference, and achieves higher accuracy than existing KV cache compression methods.

</details>


### [98] [Adaptive KappaSharp: Condition-Number Shaping for Preferential Bayesian Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07859)

Abstract: Preferential Bayesian optimization (PBO) optimizes objectives accessible only through pairwise user comparisons. The standard approach fits a Gaussian process surrogate for observed pairwise comparisons (PairwiseGP) using the Laplace approximation and selects queries with the Expected Utility of Best Option (EUBO) acquisition function. EUBO queries new candidates at each step, producing pairs that share no candidates with previous queries. Each such pair forms an isolated component in the comparison graph, removing one degree of freedom from the likelihood Hessian and making it rank-deficient. This deficiency is structural and cannot be resolved by changing the surrogate modeling approach. Existing approaches to remedy this issue either waste query budget by forcing comparisons to stay connected, or apply uniform regularization that also perturbs directions already well-constrained by the observed comparisons. We propose KappaSharp that enables a diagonal correction to the Hessian to reduce its condition number, with larger corrections where the prior uncertainty is higher. The correction is only applied in the model fitting step, not query selection. An adaptive variant of KappaSharp is also presented that activates the correction only when the surrogate is confident about recent comparisons, avoiding unnecessary corrections when the problem is well-conditioned. On 11 benchmarks (5--20 dimensions), including a 16-dimensional controller tuning problem in plasma medicine, Adaptive KappaSharp outperforms the standard PBO baseline, with up to +10.9% ($p{=}0.003$).

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：多模态情感识别（multimodal emotion recognition）；应用场景：情感计算；传感器类型：生理信号/视频/音频（AMIGOS, MAHNOB-HCI, DEAP数据集）；目标类别：情感标签（Valence, Arousal等）。与遥感目标检测无关。

Model architecture: CONFER框架：基于图（graph）的冲突感知证据协商网络。每个模态专家（modality expert）作为图节点，包含预测信念（predictive belief）、基于边界的置信度（boundary-based uncertainty）和运行时可靠性（runtime reliability）。采用不确定性感知兼容性（uncertainty-aware compatibility）和可靠性导向非对称边权重（reliability-directed asymmetric edge weights）进行迭代消息传递协商（iterative message-passing negotiation），随后进行同伴支持预测读出（peer-supported prediction readout）。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明任何轻量化方法。不涉及剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络设计等模型压缩或加速技术。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。不涉及星上/边缘部署可行性分析。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：AMIGOS, MAHNOB-HCI, DEAP（均为情感识别数据集，非遥感数据集）。评价指标：Accuracy（准确率）。实验协议：subject-dependent 10-fold交叉验证和严格leave-one-subject-out (LOSO)协议。

Experiments: 在AMIGOS-V上达到0.873准确率，在MAHNOB-V上达到0.854准确率（严格LOSO评估）。进一步分析表明：在高冲突样本上协商增益更大，对弱标签噪声具有更好的鲁棒性。

Limitations: 与遥感在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关，无法直接借鉴。摘要未说明模型复杂度、推理速度等部署相关信息。

Ideas for my research: 该论文与遥感在轨目标检测轻量化课题无直接可借鉴点。其核心贡献（多模态情感识别中的冲突感知协商机制）属于不同研究领域，无法迁移到遥感目标检测或模型轻量化任务中。

Abstract: Multimodal emotion recognition often treats self-reported labels as reliable supervision while overlooking self-report unreliability and cross-modal conflict. We propose \textbf{CONFER}, a graph-based conflict-aware evidence negotiation framework for weakly supervised multimodal emotion recognition. CONFER represents each modality expert as a node with a predictive belief, boundary-based uncertainty, and runtime reliability estimated from historical out-of-fold performance and current-sample uncertainty. Uncertainty-aware compatibility and reliability-directed asymmetric edge weights govern iterative message-passing negotiation, followed by peer-supported prediction readout. Conflict reduction, residual disagreement, and mean modality uncertainty further characterize three regimes---Consensus, Dissent, and Ambiguity---for sample-specific weak-label calibration. We evaluate CONFER on AMIGOS, MAHNOB-HCI, and DEAP under subject-dependent 10-fold and strict leave-one-subject-out (LOSO) protocols. CONFER achieves competitive performance, reaching \textbf{0.873} accuracy on AMIGOS-V and \textbf{0.854} accuracy on MAHNOB-V under strict LOSO evaluation. Further analyses show larger negotiation gains on high-conflict samples and improved robustness to weak-label corruption, indicating that cross-modal conflict provides useful information for both directional modality coordination and supervision-reliability estimation.

</details>


### [100] [V-Simba: Unleashing the Architectural Potential of RL in Visual Continuous Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07870)
*Donghu Kim,Youngdo Lee,Hojoon Lee,Johan Obando-Ceron,Byungkun Lee,Aaron Courville,Pablo Samuel Castro,Jaegul Choo,Clare Lyle*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出V-Simba，一种受状态空间RL架构Simba启发的视觉强化学习架构，通过添加归一化层和逐点卷积提升样本效率和计算效率。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文属于强化学习（RL）领域，聚焦于视觉RL的样本效率问题，与遥感、卫星、目标检测、模型轻量化均无直接或间接关联。不涉及遥感图像、目标检测任务，也不涉及模型压缩或边缘部署。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"研究方向基本无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：视觉强化学习（Visual RL）；应用场景：机器人控制（robotics）；传感器类型：视觉输入（高维图像）；目标类别：无目标检测任务。与遥感场景无关。

Model architecture: V-Simba架构，基于Soft Actor-Critic (SAC) + 数据增强，添加归一化层（normalization layers）稳定训练，使用逐点卷积（pointwise convolutions）减少计算量。

Lightweight method: 使用逐点卷积（pointwise convolutions）降低计算复杂度，属于轻量网络设计思路，但并非针对模型压缩（剪枝、量化、蒸馏等）方法。轻量化仅作为计算效率提升手段，非核心贡献。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。仅提及"computationally efficient than DrQ-v2"，但无具体数值。

Datasets and metrics: 基准测试：DMC (DeepMind Control)、Adroit、Meta-World；评价指标：样本效率（sample efficiency）、任务回报（return/reward）。与遥感目标检测数据集和指标无关。

Experiments: 在DMC、Adroit、Meta-World三个基准上，V-Simba匹配或超越SOTA方法（如DrQ-v2），且计算效率更高。摘要未提供消融实验细节。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感、卫星、目标检测完全无关；2. 轻量化仅涉及逐点卷积这一简单手段，未涉及剪枝、量化、蒸馏等主流压缩方法；3. 无部署相关指标（参数量、FLOPs、延迟等）。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，借鉴价值有限。逐点卷积（pointwise convolution）作为轻量网络设计的基本操作，在遥感目标检测轻量化中已有广泛应用（如MobileNet系列），无新意。归一化层稳定训练的思路可参考，但非核心贡献。

Abstract: Improving sample efficiency remains a core challenge in reinforcement learning (RL), especially in real-world settings like robotics, where data collection is costly. This challenge is pronounced in visual RL, where high-dimensional inputs often obscure learning signals. While prior work in visual RL has focused on algorithmic solutions, such as better dynamics models or exploration strategies, recent advances in state-based RL show that architectural design alone can lead to significant gains in sample efficiency. This raises an important question: Can these architectural principles transfer to visual RL? In response, we introduce V-Simba, a simple yet effective visual RL architecture inspired by the Simba architecture from state-based RL. Built on top of Soft Actor-Critic (SAC) with data augmentation, V-Simba modifies the architecture by adding normalization layers to stabilize training and using pointwise convolutions to reduce computation. Despite its simplicity, V-Simba matches or outperforms the state-of-the-art methods across the DMC, Adroit, and Meta-World benchmarks, while being more computationally efficient than DrQ-v2. We make our code publicly available at https://github.com/DAVIAN-Robotics/V-Simba.

</details>


### [101] [Router Sensitivity Under Lightweight Fine-Tuning Identifies Prunable Experts in Mixture-of-Experts Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07890)
*Ali Janati,Kaoutar El Maghraoui,Xinyi Luo,Wenyuan Shen,Owen Zou,Yankai Mao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种基于路由器敏感度的轻量级微调后专家剪枝方法，在MoE大语言模型上实现高效压缩，减少49%显存和37%延迟。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文聚焦于大语言模型（LLM）的MoE专家剪枝，与遥感、卫星、目标检测、轻量化部署均无直接关系。虽然剪枝方法本身属于模型压缩技术，但论文场景是自然语言处理（NLP），而非遥感图像或视觉任务，且未涉及任何遥感/卫星相关数据或应用。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向基本无关，属于NLP大模型压缩工作。除非课题涉及MoE架构的视觉模型或跨模态模型，否则不建议深入阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：MoE大语言模型的专家剪枝与轻量级微调。应用场景：NLP文本生成与推理。传感器类型：不适用。目标类别：不适用。

Model architecture: MoE架构（Mixtral-8×7B-Instruct, Qwen1.5-MoE），包含多个专家（expert）和路由器（router）。采用LoRA、IA3等参数高效微调（PEFT）方法进行轻量级适配。

Lightweight method: 专家剪枝（expert pruning）：基于路由器权重变化的ℓ2范数排序，剪除变化最小的专家。结合LoRA（低秩适配）进行轻量级微调，仅训练0.002%的参数。IA3方法冻结路由器权重。剪枝后显存减少49%，每token延迟降低37%。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明星上/边缘部署。但给出了部署相关指标：显存减少49%，每token延迟降低37%。参数量、FLOPs、能耗等未说明。模型规模为Mixtral-8×7B（约47B参数），远超出星上部署的算力预算。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：MMLU-Pro（多任务语言理解基准）、11个数学推理基准。评价指标：MMLU-Pro准确率、平均准确率、显存占用、每token延迟。

Experiments: 在Mixtral-8×7B-Instruct上，router-only LoRA（训练0.002%参数）剪除一半专家后MMLU-Pro准确率27.54%，优于全模块LoRA的24.42%。准确率随LoRA rank增加单调提升至28.76%。IA3与直接路由器适配效果相当。剪枝后准确率准线性衰减而非崩溃，最大压缩时仍比幅度剪枝或随机剪枝高约1.8倍。在Qwen1.5-MoE上，剪除一半专家后11个基准平均准确率49.7%，随机剪枝降至个位数。

Limitations: 1. 完全面向NLP大语言模型，与遥感视觉任务无关。2. 模型规模巨大（47B参数），不适合星上部署场景。3. 剪枝方法依赖MoE架构的路由器机制，无法直接迁移到CNN或标准Transformer视觉模型。4. 未讨论推理速度的绝对数值（如FPS），仅给出相对延迟降低比例。

Ideas for my research: 1. 剪枝策略的迁移思路：基于"重要性分数"（如梯度、激活值变化）进行结构化剪枝的思想可借鉴到遥感目标检测模型的通道剪枝或层剪枝。2. 轻量级微调+剪枝的联合范式：先用少量数据做参数高效微调（如LoRA），再根据微调后的变化量进行剪枝，这一范式可尝试用于遥感检测模型的轻量化。3. 路由器/门控机制：如果遥感模型采用MoE或动态稀疏结构，可借鉴本文的router-based剪枝准则。但需注意，当前主流遥感检测模型（YOLO系列、RetinaNet等）并非MoE架构。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models decouple total parameters from per-token compute, but deployment still requires storing every expert. Recent theory shows that pruning experts with the smallest router-norm changes during fine-tuning can preserve accuracy, but assumes full fine-tuning. We test whether lightweight adaptation can recover this signal. We briefly fine-tune with a parameter-efficient adapter, rank experts by the induced $\ell_2$ router change, and prune the least-changed experts in one shot. On Mixtral-8$\times$7B-Instruct (44.83% MMLU-Pro), router-only LoRA trains 0.002% of parameters and outperforms all-module LoRA at matched rank with half the experts removed (27.54% vs. 24.42%); signal quality declines as adaptation spreads to attention and expert weights. Accuracy improves monotonically with LoRA rank, reaching 28.76%. IA3, which leaves router weights frozen, matches direct router adaptation, whereas unconstrained additive adapters degrade the signal. Router-guided MMLU-Pro accuracy decays quasi-linearly rather than collapsing, remains nearly 1.8 times that of magnitude-based or random pruning at maximal compression, and reduces memory by 49% and per-token latency by 37%. At 25% compression, retention is competitive with methods using full activation statistics. The criterion also transfers to Qwen1.5-MoE fine-tuned for mathematics, retaining 49.7% mean accuracy over eleven benchmarks with half the experts removed while random pruning falls to single digits. Router sensitivity under lightweight fine-tuning therefore makes provably motivated expert pruning practical at scale.

</details>


### [102] [LLM-Based Embeddings for Program Analysis and Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07894)
*Calvin Higgins,Marco Alvarez*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次将LLMCompiler（在大规模中间表示代码上预训练的LLM）生成的程序嵌入应用于程序分析与优化任务，在算法分类和异构设备映射上取得较好结果。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文聚焦于LLM在程序分析与代码优化中的应用，与遥感、卫星、目标检测、轻量化部署完全无关。属于泛机器学习/程序分析方向，对本课题无直接参考价值。

Reading priority: 1分。与研究方向基本无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：程序分析（算法分类、异构设备映射）。场景：代码优化。与遥感目标检测无关。

Model architecture: LLMCompiler（基于LLM的预训练模型），使用源IR代码嵌入，分块嵌入后聚合为单一程序嵌入。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明。论文不涉及剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等轻量化方法。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明。不涉及星上/边缘部署、参数量、FLOPs、延迟、能耗等信息。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明具体数据集名称。评价指标：算法分类错误率（1.54%）、异构设备映射准确率。

Experiments: 算法分类错误率1.54%，比当前SOTA提升12%；异构设备映射上取得有竞争力的准确率。

Limitations: 与遥感在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关，无法提供任何直接借鉴。

Ideas for my research: 无直接可借鉴点。该论文属于程序分析与代码优化领域，与遥感在轨目标检测模型轻量化无交集。

Abstract: Recent advances have highlighted the potential of machine learning, particularly Large Language Models (LLMs), for analyzing and optimizing programs. We present the first application of program embeddings from LLMCompiler---an LLM massively pretrained on intermediate representation (IR) code---to representative program analysis and optimization tasks. We generate program embeddings directly from source and IR code using a simple approach: split programs into chunks, independently embed each chunk with pretrained LLMs, and then aggregate the chunk embeddings into a single program embedding. Our experiments show that combining source and IR code embeddings achieves an error rate of 1.54\% in algorithm classification, a 12\% improvement over the current state-of-the-art, and a competitive accuracy on heterogeneous device mapping. These findings suggest that training a performance-aware LLM for embedding IR code might yield state-of-the-art results in code optimization tasks.

</details>


### [103] [When Does Trace-Driven Evaluation Mislead MoE Expert Caching? Replay Semantics, Workload Contamination, and Operating Regimes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07911)
*Yu Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究MoE（Mixture-of-Experts）大模型在推理时将专家权重卸载到主机内存后的专家缓存管理策略评估问题，指出评估指标本身脆弱，并通过trace驱动的模拟器揭示replay语义、工作负载污染、运行区间三个评估轴会改变结论，最终论证离线最优gap被高估，轻量级因果机制实际收益有限。

Research relevance: 弱相关（2分）。本文属于泛机器学习/大模型推理系统方向，聚焦MoE模型的专家缓存管理（expert cache management）与缓存命中率策略评估，与遥感、卫星、在轨部署、目标检测均无直接关联。虽然涉及"轻量级因果机制"（lightweight causal mechanisms）和推理效率，但对象是MoE大模型推理的缓存策略，而非遥感目标检测模型的轻量化。其方法论（trace-driven模拟、评估轴分析）对实验设计有间接借鉴意义，但场景完全不相关。

Reading priority: 1分。本文与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及模型轻量化方向基本无关，属于MoE大模型推理缓存管理方向。仅评估方法论（避免指标脆弱性）有极间接借鉴意义，不构成核心参考，可不读或仅快速浏览其评估设计部分。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：MoE大模型推理时的专家缓存管理策略评估（expert cache management policy evaluation）。场景：大模型推理系统，非遥感/卫星场景。传感器类型：不适用。目标类别：不适用（对象为MoE专家权重缓存）。

Model architecture: 研究对象为三个MoE模型（40、64、128个专家），采用trace-driven、event-atomic模拟器进行离线评估。涉及缓存替换策略：LRU、LFRU、随机resident block、causal next-use predictor（因果下一使用预测器作为驱逐规则）、forced-admission oracle（强制准入预言机）。

Lightweight method: 本文并非模型压缩/轻量化方法论文。其"lightweight causal mechanisms"指轻量级因果预测机制（作为缓存驱逐规则），用于预测哪个resident expert将在未来最晚被使用。这与遥感模型轻量化（剪枝/量化/蒸馏/NAS）无关，仅名称上含"lightweight"一词。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明。本文讨论的是MoE模型推理时专家权重卸载到host memory的缓存管理，涉及内存/带宽优化，但未涉及星上/边缘部署、参数量、FLOPs、延迟、能耗等指标。与在轨部署无关。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：摘要未说明具体数据集名称。采用trace-driven模拟，基于三个MoE模型（40/64/128专家）的访问trace。指标：缓存命中率（hit rate）、miss fraction（归一化缺失率）、offline-optimal gap（离线最优差距，44.2-45.9%）、replay语义偏差（27-29% vs 4%）、early-window effect（19.4-31.9点）、最优victim选择率（causal predictor 3.4%、random 2.4%、LRU/LFRU 20.6-22.1%）。

Experiments: 主要实验发现：(1) replay语义不一致会使recency-based策略虚增27-29%，而frequency-based和static策略仅偏差4%，导致策略排名反转；(2) 工作负载污染：单指令模板probe产生逐字相同前缀，matched-pair渲染干预使early-window effect移动19.4-31.9点并反转哪些负载最cache友好；(3) 运行区间：归一化miss fraction跨模型不迁移，仅打乱事件流时间顺序使offline-optimal gap从44.9%变到30.8%；(4) 修正后稳定gap为44.2-45.9%，forced-admission oracle归因84.3-96.6%于知道哪个resident expert未来最晚被使用；(5) causal next-use predictor恢复-11.4%的gap，最优victim选择率3.4% vs random 2.4% vs LRU/LFRU 20.6-22.1%。结论：离线最优gap大幅高估了轻量级因果机制可恢复的收益。

Limitations: 摘要未说明。但可推断：本文结论基于特定MoE模型和trace设置，作者自述立场"narrow"（狭窄），仅在所评估设置下成立；未涉及遥感场景、目标检测、模型压缩或部署硬件。与本研究方向的关联性极弱。

Ideas for my research: 尽管场景无关，本文的评估方法论对"在轨遥感目标检测模型轻量化"课题有间接借鉴：(1) 强调评估指标本身可能脆弱、会改变结论——提醒我们在轻量化评估中要谨慎设计实验设置（如replay语义、数据污染、运行区间），避免指标虚增导致错误结论；(2) 指出"离线最优gap被高估"——类比到轻量化，提示我们评估压缩方法时需区分理论最优与实际可恢复收益；(3) trace-driven模拟器思想可用于在轨推理的能耗/延迟trace模拟。但整体借鉴价值有限，不建议作为核心参考。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models have outgrown accelerator memory, and offloading expert weights to host memory is now standard. This makes expert cache management an attractive lever: a policy that raised the hit rate would cut expert traffic per token. Evaluating that is a measurement problem, and we find the measurement fragile.
  With a trace-driven, event-atomic simulator over three MoE models (40, 64, 128 experts), we isolate three evaluation axes that change conclusions, not just numbers. Replay semantics: under a fused-event traffic contract, an inconsistent per-access replay inflates recency-based policies by 27-29% while leaving frequency-based and static ones within 4%, inverting the policy ranking. Workload contamination: probe sets using one instruction template per category produce verbatim-identical generation prefixes; a matched-pair rendering intervention moves the measured early-window effect by 19.4-31.9 points and reverses which workloads look most cache-friendly. Operating regimes: normalized miss fractions do not transfer across models, so the per-step expert union relative to per-layer capacity must be reported -- yet permuting only the temporal order of an identical event stream moves the offline-optimal gap from 44.9% to 30.8%, so it is not sufficient.
  Corrected, a stable gap to the offline optimum remains (44.2-45.9% over 13 frozen workload compositions). A forced-admission oracle attributes 84.3-96.6% of it to knowing which resident expert is used furthest in the future. A causal next-use predictor, used as an eviction rule, recovers -11.4% of the gap; it picks an optimal victim 3.4% of the time, against 2.4% for a random resident block and 20.6-22.1% for LRU and LFRU. Our position is narrow: in our evaluated settings a large offline-optimal gap substantially overstates the gains recovered by representative lightweight causal mechanisms.

</details>


### [104] [SPECTRA: Pushing the KV Cache Beyond the 2-Bit Cliff via Spectral Transform Coding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07915)
*Jiamu Zhang,Liang Wu,Kelly Wan,Hanjie Chen,Liangjie Hong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出SPECTRA，一种无需训练的KV cache压缩编解码器，通过将缓存旋转到由自身统计量计算出的坐标系中消除通道相关性，并将比特预算集中到承载主要信息的少数通道上，从而突破2-bit量化悬崖，在Llama-3.1-8B和Qwen2.5-7B上实现近无损4x压缩、8x仍有竞争力、最高12x压缩。

Research relevance: 弱相关（2分）。该论文属于大语言模型（LLM）推理中的KV cache压缩/量化研究，属于泛机器学习/泛视觉之外的泛NLP推理优化方向。它不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱、多光谱等任何遥感场景，也不涉及目标检测任务。虽然其核心是"量化（quantization）"这一轻量化技术，但针对的是LLM的KV cache而非CNN/Transformer检测模型，且面向GPU显存优化而非星上/边缘部署。因此与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测与模型轻量化"方向仅存在量化思想层面的弱借鉴关系。

Reading priority: 2分。该论文与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向弱相关，属于LLM KV cache量化研究。虽然其通道级非均匀比特分配和training-free压缩思想对量化策略有一定借鉴价值，但场景、任务、硬件目标均不匹配，优先级较低。仅当关注极低比特量化中outlier通道处理时值得快速浏览。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：LLM长上下文推理中的KV cache压缩。场景：agentic era下的长文档、代码库、多轮对话推理。传感器类型：不适用（非遥感）。目标类别：不适用（非目标检测）。

Model architecture: SPECTRA是一种training-free、drop-in的编解码器（codec），作用于LLM的KV cache。核心思想：将KV cache旋转（rotate）到由自身统计量计算出的坐标系中，消除通道间的强相关性，使少数通道承载几乎全部信息，从而将比特预算集中到这些关键通道上。模型为Llama-3.1-8B和Qwen2.5-7B。

Lightweight method: 核心方法是量化（quantization）与通道级比特分配（channel-wise bit allocation）。具体为：1) 通过坐标旋转（rotation）去相关化KV cache通道；2) 在旋转后的坐标系中按通道重要性分配非均匀比特预算，集中比特到信息量大的通道。属于训练无关（training-free）的模型压缩/加速方法，可视为一种混合精度量化策略。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明星上/边缘部署相关信息。摘要仅提及GPU显存优化：通过压缩KV cache使同一GPU能容纳更长上下文和更大batch。未给出参数量、FLOPs、延迟、能耗、实时性等部署指标。由于面向LLM推理且依赖GPU，与星上NPU/FPGA部署场景关联较弱。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：长上下文基准（long-context benchmarks），具体名称摘要未说明。模型：Llama-3.1-8B和Qwen2.5-7B。指标：压缩率（4x、8x、12x）及质量（near-lossless、competitive、collapsed）。具体评测指标（如准确率、困惑度等）摘要未说明。

Experiments: 在Llama-3.1-8B和Qwen2.5-7B上，SPECTRA在4x压缩时近无损（near-lossless），8x压缩时仍有竞争力（competitive，此时均匀量化已崩溃），最高可达12x压缩，将可用压缩率推过2-bit悬崖。摘要未给出与具体基线方法的详细对比表及消融实验细节。

Limitations: 1) 与遥感/卫星/目标检测完全无关，属于LLM推理优化领域。2) 摘要未说明具体长上下文基准名称、评测指标数值、参数量、FLOPs、延迟、能耗等。3) 方法针对KV cache这一LLM特有结构，难以直接迁移到CNN或检测Transformer的权重/激活压缩。4) 依赖GPU，未涉及星上NPU/FPGA等边缘硬件适配。

Ideas for my research: 1) 通道级非均匀比特分配思想可借鉴：在遥感检测模型的激活或特征图量化中，可先通过旋转/变换去相关化特征通道，再按通道重要性分配比特，避免均匀量化在outlier-heavy特征上的精度崩塌。2) "2-bit cliff"现象提示：对遥感小目标检测模型做极低比特量化时，需关注outlier通道，可借鉴其"先变换去相关、再集中比特"的策略。3) training-free、drop-in的压缩思路适合星上部署，无需重新训练即可压缩，可探索类似思路用于检测模型的特征/权重压缩。4) 旋转到统计量坐标系的方法可类比于PCA/特征分解，可用于遥感多光谱/高光谱通道的降维与比特分配。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) increasingly read long inputs in the agentic era, from whole documents and codebases to conversations across many turns. Their inference memory is then dominated by the key-value (KV) cache, the stored attention keys and values of every token the model has read and generated. Because the cache grows with context length and is re-read in full at every generated token, a longer context means more GPU memory.
  To reduce this cost, most existing methods compress the KV cache by lowering every stored value to the same low precision, a technique known as quantization. They can push this to nearly two bits per value, but rarely further, because quality drops sharply at this 2-bit cliff: four levels are too few for the cache's outlier-heavy values, where a few large entries consume the levels and collapse the rest into noise. A natural remedy is to spend more bits on the channels (feature dimensions) that matter and fewer on the rest, but the raw cache offers no handle: its channels are strongly correlated, so none stands out as more important.
  Our analysis shows that this handle appears once the cache is rotated into a coordinate system computed from its own statistics, removing these correlations. There, a small fraction of channels carries almost all the information, and spending the budget on those few is far more accurate than spreading it evenly.
  Guided by this analysis, we develop SPECTRA, a training-free, drop-in codec that re-encodes the cache into this coordinate system and concentrates the bit budget on the channels that carry the signal. On Llama-3.1-8B and Qwen2.5-7B over long-context benchmarks, SPECTRA is near-lossless at 4x compression, competitive at 8x where uniform quantization has collapsed, and reaches up to 12x, pushing usable compression past the 2-bit cliff so the same GPU holds longer contexts and larger batches.

</details>


### [105] [Information Routing across Batch Boundaries: Memory--Batch Tradeoffs in Lipschitz Bandits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07922)
*Zicheng Lyu,Zengfeng Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Summary generation failed

Research relevance: Relevance analysis unavailable

Reading priority: Priority unavailable

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: Task and scene extraction unavailable

Model architecture: Model architecture extraction unavailable

Lightweight method: Lightweight method extraction unavailable

Onboard deployability: Onboard deployability analysis unavailable

Datasets and metrics: Datasets and metrics extraction unavailable

Experiments: Experimental analysis unavailable

Limitations: Limitations analysis unavailable

Ideas for my research: Research ideas unavailable

Abstract: Adaptive learning needs both a state that preserves what observations imply and opportunities to act on that state. We study this width--depth tradeoff in stochastic Lipschitz bandits. After each pull, the learner retains at most $W$ bits of live reward-dependent state and organizes its pulls into at most $B$ committed batches. For $W\gtrsim_d\log(eT)$, we characterize minimax expected pseudo-regret up to logarithmic factors; the lower bounds hold for every $W$. Besides the classical sequential and unrestricted-memory batch costs, the frontier contains the new penalty \[
  T^{\frac{d+2}{d+3}}
  \bigl(1+(B-1)W\bigr)^{-\frac1{d(d+3)}}, \] proving that state width and update depth are not interchangeable. The interaction is an information-routing constraint: at regional scale $s$, low regret forces the committed action transcript to encode $Θ_d(s^{-d})$ regional decisions, while the collected boundary states carry at most $(B-1)W$ bits of entropy. Matching policies stream and erase verification statistics while retaining a mask of a safe active set, either in memory or fragment by fragment. The theorem recovers the full-dimensional worst-case batch-only frontier and logarithmic-memory achievability in the fully sequential specialization; static batch boundaries match predictable adaptive ones.

</details>


### [106] [Spectral Outliers Reveal Dominant Learned Structure in Transformer Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07921)
*Kasun Dewage,Marianna Pensky,Suranadi De Silva,T. H. Bandara*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 利用Marchenko-Pastur随机矩阵理论对预训练注意力权重进行谱分解，将投影矩阵分为随机噪声和谱异常值两部分，并验证了谱异常值编码了学习结构的主要成分，为结构化剪枝和参数高效微调提供理论依据。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文与遥感、卫星、在轨部署、目标检测均无直接关系，属于大语言模型（LLM）注意力机制的理论分析工作，不涉及遥感图像、目标检测或轻量化部署方法。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测轻量化方向基本无关，属于LLM理论分析，仅谱分解思路有微弱借鉴可能，不建议优先阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：注意力权重谱分析；应用场景：大语言模型（LLM）预训练Transformer；传感器类型：不适用；目标类别：不适用。

Model architecture: 分析对象为预训练Transformer（如Mistral-7B等11个模型），关注Q/K/V/O投影矩阵的谱结构，涉及grouped-query attention。

Lightweight method: 论文末尾提及该谱分解方法可指导parameter-efficient fine-tuning和structured pruning，但未提出具体的轻量化方法或算法，仅给出理论观察。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。该工作不涉及星上/边缘部署。

Datasets and metrics: 验证任务：HellaSwag、MMLU、PIQA（均为NLP基准数据集）；评价指标：随机基线性能对比（zeroing异常值后性能接近随机猜测）。

Experiments: 在Mistral-7B上验证：清零MP识别的谱异常值（信号）后，HellaSwag/MMLU/PIQA性能降至随机水平；清零等数量的bulk奇异值则造成较小但不可忽略的退化。在11个预训练Transformer上识别出5种重复模式。

Limitations: 1. 仅针对NLP领域的LLM，未涉及视觉或遥感任务；2. 未提出具体的剪枝/量化/蒸馏算法，仅给出理论观察；3. 未验证在遥感目标检测模型上的有效性；4. 未讨论计算开销或部署可行性。

Ideas for my research: 1. 可将MP随机矩阵理论引入遥感目标检测模型的注意力权重分析，识别遥感Transformer中哪些注意力成分是信号、哪些是噪声；2. 该谱分解方法可能为遥感检测模型的结构化剪枝提供理论指导，即优先剪除bulk（噪声）部分，保留谱异常值（信号）；3. 但需注意该工作针对NLP注意力，遥感视觉Transformer的注意力谱结构可能不同，需重新验证。

Abstract: We apply Marchenko-Pastur (MP) random matrix theory to pre-trained attention weights in order to separate each projection matrix into a random-like bulk and a set of spectral outliers. We validate this decomposition causally: zeroing the MP-identified outliers (signal) in Mistral-7B drives HellaSwag, MMLU, and PIQA close to random-chance performance, whereas zeroing a count-matched subset of bulk singular values causes smaller but non-negligible degradation. Across 11 pre-trained transformers we identify five recurring patterns: spectral outliers encode a dominant component of the learned structure; Q projections carry the most outliers; V projections under grouped-query attention lack a clean signal/noise separation; entry-level outliers form structured row-bands in Q and column-bands in O; and specific residual-stream dimensions persist as band outliers across layers in K and O. We close by outlining how these observations could inform parameter-efficient fine-tuning and structured pruning.

</details>


### [107] [Causal State-Space Model for Causal Inference: Estimating Longitudinal Individual Treatment Effects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08288)
*Abisoye Abidakun,Mingjun Zhong,Georgios Leontidis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出CSSD和CSSPD两个因果状态空间模型，通过对比预测编码和局部信息最大化解决领域混淆导致的互信息冲突，用于纵向观测数据的反事实结果估计。

Research relevance: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向基本无关。论文聚焦临床决策支持中的因果推断（counterfactual outcome estimation），属于医疗时间序列/因果机器学习领域，不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱、多光谱或空间在轨智能。也不涉及目标检测、小目标检测、旋转框检测或遥感场景理解。轻量化方面仅提及O(T)编码器复杂度，但并非面向边缘/星上部署的模型压缩方法。相关性评分：1分。

Reading priority: 1分。论文属于临床因果推断领域，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向基本无关，仅SSM高效编码和对比预测正则化思想有极间接的参考价值，不建议作为核心阅读材料。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：纵向观测数据上的反事实结果估计（counterfactual outcome estimation），用于临床决策支持。场景：医疗/临床时间序列。传感器类型：不适用（非遥感）。目标类别：不适用（非目标检测）。

Model architecture: CSSD（Causal State-Space model with Direct decoder）：采用选择性状态空间模型（selective State Space Models, 类似Mamba），配并行多步解码器（parallel multi-step decoder），单次前向同时产生所有预测时域，消除累积rollout误差。CSSPD：在CSSD基础上增加Contrastive Predictive Coding（对比预测编码）和Local Information Maximisation（局部信息最大化），以增强平衡表示中的时间可预测性并恢复被领域混淆破坏的局部协变量信息。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明传统轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS等均未涉及）。仅提及CSSD/CSSPD以O(T)编码器成本运行，相比Causal Transformer在编码复杂度上更高效，但这属于模型架构层面的效率优势，并非面向星上/边缘部署的模型压缩手段。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等部署指标。仅提及O(T)编码器复杂度，无任何面向星上或边缘部署的讨论。该模型面向临床决策支持，与在轨遥感部署场景无关。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：MIMIC-III（临床重症监护数据库）和Cancer Simulation（癌症模拟数据，混淆强度gamma∈{0,1,2,3,4}）。评价指标：counterfactual RMSE（反事实均方根误差）。

Experiments: 在MIMIC-III上，CSSPD在每个时域tau>=2上均取得比Causal Transformer更低的counterfactual RMSE，增益从0.02（2步）到0.07（6步）。在Cancer Simulation上，CSSPD在gamma<=3时优于CT（margin 25.9%–37.0%），CSSD取得最低总体平均RMSE（较CT降低12.7%），验证了MI冲突分析。

Limitations: 摘要未说明模型参数量、推理速度、部署资源等关键信息。论文完全面向临床因果推断，与遥感/卫星/目标检测/轻量化部署无任何交集。其状态空间模型（SSM）架构虽在序列建模上高效，但未针对遥感图像目标检测设计，也未涉及任何视觉检测任务。

Ideas for my research: 借鉴意义有限，但有两处可间接参考：(1) 选择性状态空间模型（SSM/Mamba类）在长序列建模上的O(T)高效编码思路，可考虑用于遥感视频/时序遥感数据的轻量特征提取；(2) 对比预测编码（Contrastive Predictive Coding）与信息最大化正则化在表示学习中的思想，或可迁移到遥感目标检测的特征蒸馏/表示学习中。但需注意，本文不涉及视觉目标检测，迁移需自行设计。

Abstract: Estimating counterfactual outcomes over time from longitudinal observational data is central to clinical decision support. Existing methods rely on domain confusion -- adversarial training that renders representations invariant to treatment assignment -- yet this invariance creates a mutual information conflict: it suppresses treatment-correlated covariate signals necessary for accurate outcome prediction. We formalise this tension via a Jensen-Shannon divergence bound on counterfactual prediction error and develop two complementary models. CSSD (Causal State-Space model with Direct decoder) adapts selective State Space Models with a parallel multi-step decoder that eliminates accumulated rollout error by producing all prediction horizons simultaneously in a single forward pass. CSSPD (Causal State-Space model with Predictive regularisation and Direct decoder) augments CSSD with Contrastive Predictive Coding and Local Information Maximisation to reinforce temporal predictability in the balancing representation and recover local covariate information destroyed by domain confusion. On MIMIC-III, CSSPD achieves lower counterfactual RMSE than the Causal Transformer at every horizon tau >= 2 at O(T) encoder cost, with gains from 0.02 (2-step) to 0.07 (6-step). On Cancer Simulation across confounding strengths gamma in {0,1,2,3,4}, CSSPD outperforms CT at gamma <= 3 (margins 25.9%--37.0%), and CSSD achieves the lowest overall average RMSE (12.7% reduction over CT), confirming the MI conflict analysis. To our knowledge, this is the first work to formalise the balancing-prediction MI conflict and propose a structured resolution through complementary predictive and information-theoretic training objectives.

</details>


### [108] [Exact Rank and Convex Calibration Dimension Lower Bounds for the Multi-Label F1 Loss](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08399)
*Mingyuan Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从数学上严格证明了多标签分类中F1分数损失矩阵的秩为s²-s+2，并给出了凸校准替代损失函数维度的二次下界，建立了CCdim(L^F1)=Θ(s²)的渐近紧界。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。该论文是纯数学理论工作，研究多标签分类中F1度量的损失矩阵秩和凸校准替代函数的维度下界，完全不涉及遥感图像、卫星数据、目标检测或模型轻量化中的任何内容。

Reading priority: 1分。纯数学理论论文，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及模型轻量化研究方向完全无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 摘要未说明。论文任务为多标签分类中的F1度量理论分析，不涉及遥感场景、传感器类型或目标类别。

Model architecture: 摘要未说明。论文不涉及任何神经网络模型结构或网络设计。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明。论文不涉及剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS或任何轻量化方法。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明。论文不涉及参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗或任何部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明。论文不涉及任何实验数据集或实验设置。

Limitations: 该论文是纯理论数学工作，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化完全无关，无法提供任何工程或算法层面的借鉴。

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴意义。该论文与"在轨遥感目标检测模型轻量化"课题方向完全无关，不涉及遥感、目标检测、轻量化或部署中的任何技术点。

Abstract: The instance-wise $F_1$ measure is a central performance measure for multi-label classification. For a problem with $s$ labels, it defines a $2^s\times 2^s$ loss matrix. Previous work exhibited $s^2+1$-coordinate affine and shifted low-rank representations and used them to construct quadratic-dimensional convex calibrated surrogates. We determine the exact rank. Under the convention $F_1(\varnothing,\varnothing)=1$, the $F_1$ score matrix, the shifted loss matrix, and the unshifted loss matrix all have rank $s^2-s+2$, while the column-affine dimension of the loss is $s^2-s+1$. The proof factors the nonempty score matrix through subset-incidence matrices and a positive-definite Cauchy matrix.
  Exact rank does not, by itself, lower-bound the dimension of an arbitrary convex calibrated surrogate. We therefore analyze the Bayes geometry of $F_1$ directly. We construct a distribution for which precisely all supersets of a fixed core label set are Bayes optimal, and show that the corresponding active loss columns, restricted to the witness support, have affine dimension $hn$, where $n=s-\lfloor s/3\rfloor$ and $h=\lceil(s\lfloor s/3\rfloor)^{1/2}\rceil-1$. Applying the feasible-subspace lower bound for convex calibration dimension gives \[
  \operatorname{CCdim}(L^{F_1})
  \ge \left(\frac{2}{3\sqrt{3}}-o(1)\right)s^2. \] Together with the quadratic upper bound, this establishes $\operatorname{CCdim}(L^{F_1})=Θ(s^2)$.

</details>


### [109] [Second Order Drifting Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07924)
*Drake Brown,Yuhao Huang,Shih-Hsin Wang,Bao Wang*

TL;DR: 提出二阶漂移模型，通过将生成过程提升到相空间并引入人工速度变量，缓解一阶漂移模型在傅里叶空间中频谱刚度导致的细尺度结构恢复缓慢问题，保留一步推理能力。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：生成式建模（distribution matching, sequential data generation, robotic control）。应用场景：合成数据分布匹配、时序数据生成、机器人控制。传感器类型：不涉及。目标类别：不涉及。

Model architecture: 二阶漂移模型（Second-Order Drifting Models）：将一阶漂移动力学提升到相空间，引入人工速度变量，使密度扰动在傅里叶空间中服从加速二阶动力学，与Nesterov加速优化理论建立联系。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明任何轻量化方法。论文核心是改进生成模型的训练动力学，而非模型压缩或加速。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。虽然声称保留一步推理（one-step inference），但讨论的是生成模型，非目标检测模型，且未涉及星上/边缘部署。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明具体数据集名称和评价指标。仅提及在synthetic distribution matching, sequential data generation, robotic control三个场景下评估。

Experiments: 在合成分布匹配、序列数据生成、机器人控制三个任务上，二阶漂移模型相比一阶漂移基线在收敛行为上有所改善，性能达到或优于基线。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化完全无关；2. 仅在生成模型领域验证，未涉及检测任务；3. 未讨论任何模型压缩、推理加速或边缘部署问题；4. 实验场景有限，未在真实遥感数据上验证。

Ideas for my research: 该论文与"在轨遥感目标检测模型轻量化"课题基本无关，无可借鉴点。二阶动力学加速的思想虽在优化理论中有价值，但应用于遥感目标检测轻量化场景缺乏直接联系。

Abstract: Drifting models are a recent class of one-step generative models that evolve the model distribution during training using a predefined sample-based drift field. Although they avoid iterative inference, their kernel-based drift fields induce frequency-dependent training dynamics: In the linearized regime, each Fourier mode of the density residual decays at a rate determined by the kernel spectrum, leading to slow recovery of fine-scale structure. We propose Second-Order Drifting Models, which lift drifting dynamics into phase space by augmenting generated samples with artificial velocity variables. We show that the resulting density perturbations obey accelerated second-order dynamics in Fourier space, connecting drifting models to the celebrated Nesterov acceleration from optimization theory. This provides a principled mechanism for mitigating the spectral stiffness of first-order drifting while preserving one-step inference. We derive a practical semi-implicit training algorithm and evaluate it on synthetic distribution matching, sequential data generation, and robotic control. Across these settings, the second-order drifting model improves convergence behavior and achieves competitive or superior performance over first-order drifting baselines.

</details>

### [110] [Generalizing deep reinforcement learning across cable-driven parallel robot configurations with actuator-level policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07546)
*Abir Bouaouda,Mohamed Boutayeb,François Charpillet,Dominique Martinez,Rémi Pannequin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于深度强化学习的执行器级策略，用于控制缆索驱动机器人，不依赖具体机器人配置。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文与遥感、卫星、目标检测、轻量化完全无关，属于机器人控制领域（缆索驱动机器人+深度强化学习），不涉及任何遥感图像处理、目标检测或模型压缩/加速技术。

Reading priority: 1分。与研究方向完全无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：机器人控制（缆索驱动机器人CDPR控制）；应用场景：工业机器人；传感器类型：未涉及；目标类别：未涉及。与遥感目标检测无关。

Model architecture: 执行器级策略（Actuator-Level Policy, ALP），基于深度强化学习（DRL），具体网络结构摘要未说明。

Lightweight method: 摘要未涉及任何轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。不涉及星上/边缘部署。

Datasets and metrics: 训练在仿真环境中进行，真实实验使用8电机3D运动CDPR和4电机2D平面CDPR。评价指标：鲁棒性（robustness）和精度（precision），具体数值摘要未说明。

Experiments: 在仿真中训练，迁移到真实CDPR。ALP在鲁棒性和精度上超越传统强化学习方法。跨配置迁移成功：在仿真4电机2D平面CDPR上训练的策略可直接控制真实8电机3D CDPR。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关，无任何可借鉴的遥感/检测/轻量化内容。

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴意义。该论文属于机器人控制领域，不涉及遥感图像、目标检测或模型轻量化中的任何一项核心技术。

Abstract: Cable-driven parallel robots (CDPRs) present diverse configurations and complex control challenges, which can be addressed by deep reinforcement learning (DRL) by learning their nonlinear dynamics. However, DRL methods often require extensive training time, and the resulting policies do not generalize well to different robot configurations or varying numbers of actuators. In this article, we introduce a novel DRL approach for controlling CDPRs that does not depend on the specific robot configuration. Our method trains an actuator-level policy that controls each motor to achieve its target cable length, in contrast to conventional DRL approaches that learn to control the entire robot to reach a desired end-effector position. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first work to apply DRL to control CDPRs using an actuator-level policy. This approach offers two main advantages: (i) a single shared policy can be applied to any CDPR configuration, regardless of actuator count, and (ii) reliance on inverse kinematics, avoiding the more challenging forward kinematics problem. Training is performed in simulation, and the learned policy is successfully transferred to a real CDPR. Experimental results show that the actuator-level policy (ALP) surpasses traditional reinforcement learning methods in both robustness and precision. We further control a real 8-motor CDPR with 3D motion using a policy trained on a simulated 4-motor planar CDPR operating in 2D. This illustrates that the proposed method is applicable to any CDPR configuration, independent of actuator number or placement.

</details>


### [111] [SC$^{2}$-WM: A Self-Correcting World Model with Closed-Loop Feedback for Vision-and-Language Navigation in Continuous Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07548)
*Xuan Yao,Yuze Zhu,Junyu Gao,Zongmeng Wang,Changsheng Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出SC²-WM自校正世界模型框架，通过内部反馈实现VLN-CE连续环境下的视觉-语言导航闭环决策，包含状态级和模型级两级校正机制。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。该论文研究的是Vision-and-Language Navigation in Continuous Environments (VLN-CE)，属于室内/连续环境下的视觉-语言导航任务，不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱、多光谱、空间在轨智能等任何遥感相关场景，也不涉及目标检测或模型轻量化。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及模型轻量化研究方向基本无关，不建议投入时间阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：视觉-语言导航（VLN-CE）；应用场景：连续环境下的室内导航；传感器类型：视觉（RGB）；目标类别：无目标检测任务。与遥感目标检测完全无关。

Model architecture: SC²-WM框架包含世界模型（world model），用于内部反馈和闭环决策。包含状态级规划修正（state-level plan refinement）和模型级条件世界感知自适应（conditional world-aware adaptation）。摘要未说明具体网络结构细节。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。摘要未提及剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络、实时推理、边缘部署、星上部署、FPGA/NPU/TensorRT等任何轻量化或模型压缩方法。

Onboard deployability: 不涉及。摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等任何部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 标准VLN-CE基准数据集（摘要未说明具体数据集名称）；评价指标为导航鲁棒性和泛化性（摘要未说明具体指标名称如成功率、路径长度等）。

Experiments: 在标准VLN-CE基准上进行了实验，证明了改进的导航鲁棒性和泛化性。摘要未提供与基线方法的定量对比数据。

Limitations: 与遥感在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关，无直接借鉴意义。摘要未说明计算开销、推理速度等实际部署约束。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关。虽然该论文的世界模型自校正思想（内部反馈闭环、测试时自适应更新）在理论上可迁移到其他领域，但与本课题（遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化）无直接关联。不建议深入阅读。

Abstract: Vision-and-Language Navigation in Continuous Environments (VLN-CE) requires agents to make fine-grained navigation decisions under partial observability. However, most existing methods rely on open-loop execution, lacking mechanisms to detect and correct internal state drift during inference. We propose SC$^{2}$-WM, a self-correcting world model framework that introduces internal feedback for closed-loop decision making in VLN-CE. Our method derives feedback from world-model foresight to perform state-level plan refinement before action execution. To handle challenging scenarios, we further introduce conditional world-aware adaptation, which enables model-level correction by selectively updating the world model at test time when feedback indicates model capacity insufficiency. Experiments on standard VLN-CE benchmarks demonstrate improved navigation robustness and generalization. Our code is available at https://github.com/sunrise-ikun/SC2_WM.

</details>


### [112] [Self Supervised Learning from Automatically Generated Demonstrations for Visual Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07553)
*Andres Rivas,Anselmo R. Cukla,Rodrigo S. Guerra,Bruna V. Guterres,Ricardo B. Grando*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种自监督视觉机械臂操作方法，利用ROS~2和Isaac Sim自动生成演示数据，训练CNN从单帧RGB图像回归相对位姿，实现粗到精的抓取控制。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文研究的是机器人视觉抓取操作（robotic manipulation），与遥感卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱/多光谱、空间在轨智能完全无关；不涉及目标检测、小目标检测、旋转框检测、遥感场景理解；不涉及轻量化、剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS、实时推理、边缘/星上部署、FPGA/NPU/TensorRT。属于泛机器人视觉方法，与本研究方向基本无关。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：机器人视觉抓取（visual manipulation, grasp）。场景：仿真环境（Isaac Sim）和真实UR5e协作机器人。传感器：腕部单目RGB相机。目标类别：三个物理物体（未具体说明类别）。

Model architecture: 卷积网络（convolutional network），用于从单帧RGB图像回归相对平移和旋转（relative translation and rotation）。具体网络结构摘要未说明。

Lightweight method: 摘要未涉及任何轻量化方法。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：自动生成的仿真数据集（平面精调数据集和三维粗逼近数据集）。评价指标：平面分散度（planar dispersion, mm）、抓取成功率（success rate, %）。

Experiments: 仿真实验：精调阶段将平面分散度从9.69 mm降至5.38 mm。真实实验：对三个物理物体进行端到端抓取，两个物体在无旋转条件下成功率为66.6%和63.6%，旋转条件下仍保持部分鲁棒性。

Limitations: 深度预测（depth prediction）存在挑战；物体依赖的泛化性（object dependent generalization）不足；仅在三个物体上验证，泛化能力有限。

Ideas for my research: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化课题无直接可借鉴点。若强行关联，其"自动生成标注数据"的思路可启发遥感领域利用仿真平台（如Blender、Unity）自动生成带标注的遥感图像用于训练轻量化检测模型，但该论文本身不涉及遥感或检测。

Abstract: Robotic manipulation often requires object specific programming, manual data annotation, or calibrated perception pipelines, which limits rapid deployment in practical settings. Learning from demonstration offers a more direct alternative, but collecting demonstrations can still demand human teleoperation or kinesthetic teaching. This paper presents a self supervised visual manipulation method in which a robot automatically generates demonstrations around a target pose and learns relative pose corrections directly from wrist mounted RGB images. The proposed pipeline uses ROS~2 and Isaac Sim to collect labeled image-pose pairs without requiring explicit camera to robot extrinsic calibration. Separate datasets are generated for planar refinement and coarse three dimensional approach, and a convolutional network is trained to regress relative translation and rotation from single frame RGB observations. During execution, a coarse to fine controller first approaches the object using models trained with height variation and then refines the final alignment using planar data. The method is evaluated both in simulation and on a real UR5e collaborative robot equipped with a gripper and a monocular camera. In simulation, the refinement stage reduces the final planar dispersion from 9.69 mm to 5.38 mm. In real world experiments, the system performs end to end grasp attempts on three physical objects and reaches success rates of 66.6% and 63.6% for two objects without object rotation, while still maintaining partial robustness under rotated conditions. These results show that automatically generated demonstrations can support practical visual manipulation with limited setup effort, while also exposing remaining challenges in depth prediction and object dependent generalization.

</details>


### [113] [You Don't Need To Stay in The Loop: An Agentic Robotics Loop for Robot-Policy Improvement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07555)
*Hang Yu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 AgenticRobotics，一个后端无关的控制平面，将 coding agent 的多智能体架构移植到机器人策略改进，通过 LLM 控制器驱动可持久化的 train-evaluate-improve 事务，实现证据门控的模型提升、崩溃恢复与工具质量度量。

Research relevance: 弱相关（1-2 分）。论文主题是机器人策略改进（robot-policy improvement）的 agentic 控制平面，与遥感、卫星、在轨目标检测、模型轻量化均无直接关系。它不涉及 remote sensing、object detection、pruning、quantization、knowledge distillation、FLOPs 等任何本方向核心概念。唯一可能的间接借鉴是"工具质量度量、证据门控、崩溃恢复"这类工程化流程思想，但并非视觉/检测/轻量化方法本身。

Reading priority: 1 分。论文与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及模型轻量化方向基本无关，属于机器人策略改进的 agentic 控制平面工作。仅对"模型在线更新/版本管理的可靠性流程"有极间接的工程思想借鉴，不涉及任何检测算法或模型压缩方法，不建议优先精读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：机器人策略改进（robot-policy improvement）的自动化控制流程。场景：机器人训练与评估循环，非遥感/卫星场景。传感器类型：摘要未说明。目标类别：不适用（非目标检测任务）。

Model architecture: AgenticRobotics 采用多智能体架构：主 agent（main agent）管理循环，subagents 负责分析与执行，tools 执行具体工作。核心组件包括：不可变目标（immutable objective）、控制器拥有的测量（controller-owned measurement）、commit-keyed 崩溃恢复、证据分级技能库（evidence-graded skill library）、带标准化记录调用面的工具注册表（tool registry）。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明。论文不涉及任何模型压缩或加速方法（无 pruning、quantization、distillation、NAS、轻量网络等）。其"轻量化"含义是流程层面的控制面设计，而非模型轻量化。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明。论文不涉及星上/边缘部署，未给出参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等任何部署指标。其关注的是训练/评估流程的可靠性（false-promotion control、crash recovery、artifact tampering 检测），而非推理部署。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明具体数据集。评价指标包括：false-promotion control（0.001 per run hardened 对比 0.005–0.021 shipped）、anytime-valid decisions under optional stopping、kill injection 下零丢失/零重复效果、六类 artifact-tampering 全部被 signed verifier 捕获。这些是流程可靠性指标，非检测精度指标（无 mAP、IoU 等）。

Experiments: 摘要给出流程可靠性实验：false-promotion 率从 shipped 的 0.005–0.021 硬化到 0.001 per run；在 optional stopping 下提供 anytime-valid 决策；kill injection 下零丢失或重复效果；六种 artifact 篡改类别全部被 signed verifier 捕获。作者明确说明标题是操作性声明而非选择性声明——在测量的单一 lineage 上，该循环并未比人类挑选出更好的 checkpoint。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感/卫星/目标检测/轻量化完全无关，属于机器人策略改进的 agentic 工程框架。2. 作者自述在单一 lineage 上并未比人类挑选出更好的 checkpoint，即提升效果有限。3. 摘要未说明模型架构细节、数据集、计算资源、部署指标。4. 属于流程/系统层面工作，不提供任何视觉检测或模型压缩方法。

Ideas for my research: 尽管与课题弱相关，但可借鉴其工程化思想：1) "工具质量必须每次调用时度量并记录，artifact 变化时过期"——可类比到在轨模型更新/增量学习场景，对模型版本进行证据门控与回滚管理。2) "commit-keyed crash recovery" 与"证据门控提升"思想可用于星上模型在线更新的可靠性保障（防止错误权重固化）。3) "signed verifier 捕获 artifact 篡改"可借鉴为模型/权重完整性校验。但这些均为流程管理层面的间接启发，不提供检测或轻量化算法本身。

Abstract: Coding agents such as Claude Code and Codex close the software loop: a main agent manages the loop, subagents analyze and execute, tools do the work. We port this architecture to robot-policy improvement, where one difference dominates the design: robotic tools---trained policies, training pipelines, data collection---fail routinely, so a tool's quality must be measured, recorded at every call, and expired when the artifact behind it changes. AgenticRobotics is a backend-independent control plane in which an LLM controller drives disposable workers through durable train--evaluate--improve transactions: an immutable objective, controller-owned measurement, commit-keyed crash recovery, an evidence-graded skill library, and a tool registry with a standardized, recorded call surface. The title is an operational claim, not a selection claim: the operator can leave because promotion is evidence-gated, state is recoverable, and capability quality is derived from records---not because the loop picks better checkpoints than a human; on the one lineage we measured, it does not. The gates measurably buy false-promotion control (0.001 per run hardened versus 0.005--0.021 shipped), anytime-valid decisions under optional stopping, zero lost or duplicate effects under kill injection, and six of six artifact-tampering classes caught by a signed verifier.

</details>


### [114] [AeroDPO: Unleashing Lightweight UAV Navigation with High-Fidelity Perception and Automated Preference Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07557)
*Peng Xu,Chengcheng Wang,Shaohua Wan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出AeroDPO，一种零成本自动直接偏好优化（DPO）流水线，通过物理仿真状态回滚生成碰撞规避偏好数据，使轻量2B视觉语言模型在UAV-VLN任务上达到与7B大模型相当的成功率并显著降低碰撞率。

Research relevance: 弱相关（2分）。论文涉及无人机（UAV）视觉语言导航，属于航空/边缘部署场景，且关注轻量化模型（2B vs 7B）与边缘部署延迟，但核心任务是Vision-Language Navigation（视觉语言导航），并非遥感/卫星目标检测。不涉及遥感图像、SAR、高光谱、多光谱、在轨智能或目标检测。轻量化体现在模型参数规模（2B）而非剪枝/量化/蒸馏等压缩技术，且场景为无人机导航而非卫星在轨遥感。

Reading priority: 2分。论文与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向弱相关，核心是无人机视觉语言导航。但其"轻量模型+高保真输入"的洞察和自动数据飞轮思想有一定借鉴价值，可作为方法论参考，非核心必读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：Vision-Language Navigation (UAV-VLN)，即无人机视觉语言导航。场景：复杂3D环境中的自主导航与避障。传感器：视觉输入（高保真视觉图像）。目标类别：导航路径与避障决策，非目标检测。摘要未说明具体传感器类型（如是否含遥感载荷）。

Model architecture: 采用端到端（end-to-end）minimalist范式，基于视觉语言模型（VLM）。核心对比为轻量2B模型 vs 大规模7B模型。关键洞察：感知质量（perception quality）比语言推理能力（language reasoning capacity）更重要。AeroDPO包含：确定性物理仿真状态回滚（state rollback）、解耦特权干预（decoupled privileged interventions）、离线视觉语言检查器（offline vision language inspector）。

Lightweight method: 轻量化体现在模型参数规模选择（2B轻量模型替代7B大模型），而非传统模型压缩技术。摘要未说明使用剪枝（pruning）、量化（quantization）、知识蒸馏（knowledge distillation）或NAS。核心轻量化思路是"感知质量优于语言推理容量"，即用更小模型+高保真视觉输入达到同等性能。摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟等具体数值。

Onboard deployability: 关注边缘部署（edge deployment），指出7B大模型存在prohibitive latency（过高延迟）。采用2B轻量模型以降低延迟。但摘要未说明具体延迟数值、参数量、FLOPs、显存、能耗、帧率等部署指标。未涉及FPGA/NPU/TensorRT等硬件加速。场景为无人机边缘部署，非卫星在轨部署。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明具体数据集名称。评价指标：success rates（成功率，unmapped scenarios上达49.16%）、collision rates（碰撞率）。涉及out-of-distribution (OOD)场景评估。摘要未说明具体benchmark名称。

Experiments: 跨尺度评估（cross-scale evaluations）显示2B模型+高保真视觉输入与7B基线成功率完全匹配。AeroDPO在unmapped scenarios上将成功率提升至49.16%，并大幅抑制碰撞率，建立autonomous aerial agents的新SOTA。摘要未说明具体对比方法、消融实验细节。

Limitations: 1. 核心任务为视觉语言导航，与遥感卫星目标检测无直接关联。2. 轻量化仅体现在模型规模选择，未涉及模型压缩技术。3. 摘要未说明具体部署指标（延迟、FLOPs、能耗）。4. 未涉及遥感图像、SAR、高光谱、多光谱等遥感数据。5. 场景为无人机低空导航，非卫星在轨遥感。

Ideas for my research: 1. "感知质量优于语言推理容量"这一洞察可迁移：在遥感目标检测中，可探索用更轻量骨干网络+高质量输入（如超分、多尺度特征）替代大模型，实现轻量化。2. AeroDPO的"零成本自动数据飞轮"思想可借鉴：通过仿真/物理模拟自动生成难例与偏好数据，用于遥感小目标检测的难例挖掘或数据增强。3. 基于物理仿真状态回滚生成负样本/拒绝样本的思路，可用于遥感检测中的OOD鲁棒性增强。4. 2B vs 7B的跨尺度评估方法论可借鉴，用于验证轻量模型在遥感检测上的性能边界。

Abstract: Vision-Language Navigation for Unmanned Aerial Vehicles (UAV-VLN) requires rapid and reactive control in complex 3D environments. Recent minimalist end-to-end paradigms show great promise but typically rely on massive language models containing billions of parameters, incurring prohibitive latency for real-world edge deployment. In this paper, we challenge this parameter-heavy reliance. Comprehensive cross-scale evaluations reveal the critical insight that perception quality fundamentally outweighs language reasoning capacity. We demonstrate that a lightweight 2B model equipped with high-fidelity visual inputs completely matches the overall success rates of massive 7B baselines. However, this minimalist policy exposes a fundamental robustness flaw inherent to pure Behavior Cloning (BC). Lacking explicit negative feedback, the agent fails to internalize robust spatial constraints and exhibits alarming collision rates in out-of-distribution (OOD) scenarios. To overcome this vulnerability without relying on unscalable human annotations, we propose AeroDPO, a zero-cost automated Direct Preference Optimization pipeline driven by deterministic physical simulation state rollback. Upon detecting collisions, the system autonomously rewinds the environment to extract causal reasoning errors as rejected actions, applies decoupled privileged interventions to synthesize collision-avoidance preferred maneuvers, and leverages an offline vision language inspector to filter visual ambiguities. By equipping our 2B model with this automated data flywheel, AeroDPO boosts success rates to 49.16% on unmapped scenarios while drastically suppressing collision rates, establishing a new SOTA for autonomous aerial agents.

</details>


### [115] [Learning Physical Interaction: A Survey of Tactile- and Force-aware Robot Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07558)
*Shilin Shan,Chuhao Zhou,Ruize Wang,Xinyan Chen,Xiangyu Chen,Xinyu Zhou,Boyu Ma,Iris Yuxuan Hu,Jingliang Li,Celeste Yuxuan Hu,Geng Li,Guohao Chen,Tianrui Zhu,Zhe Li,Yanjie Ze,Haoran Geng,Zhiyang Dou,Jianxin Bi,Yuejiang Liu,Jianshu Zhou,Jiachen Li,Paul Liang,Tatsuya Harada,Robert Katzschmann,Harold Soh,Na Li,Edward Johns,Danica Kragic,Jan Peters,Wojciech Matusik,Masayoshi Tomizuka,Jitendra Malik,Jianfei Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出TF-ART，一个面向力觉/触觉感知机器人学习的统一分类法，从多模态感知与多阶段系统设计角度综述相关方法。

<details>

Ideas for my research: 弱相关。唯一可借鉴之处在于其"多阶段架构"（高层策略+动作精化+低层控制）的思想，可类比为遥感在轨检测中"粗检测+精化+后处理"的级联设计思路；以及多模态感知融合（视觉+力觉）可类比遥感多光谱/高光谱多模态融合。但整体与本研究方向关联度极低。

Abstract: Physically grounded robot intelligence requires robots to perceive, reason about, and regulate their interactions with the physical world. This capability is particularly critical in contact-sensitive manipulation, where successful task execution depends not only on visual perception and motion generation, but also on force regulation and adaptive control. In this context, recent robot learning methods have made substantial progress by integrating force, tactile, vision, language, and proprioceptive sensing into learned manipulation policies. In parallel, many systems adopt multi-phase architectures that combine high-level policies, action-refinement modules, and low-level controllers to bridge semantic task understanding with reactive physical execution. Despite these advances, existing surveys have not explicitly reviewed force- and tactile-aware robot learning from a unified perspective that jointly captures multimodal sensing and multi-phase system design. This survey addresses this gap by proposing TF-ART, a Tactile/Force-Aware Robot learning Taxonomy for multimodal and multi-phase frameworks, which maps individual methods into a unified hierarchical structure. The framework characterizes how recent works organize observation modalities, encode and fuse heterogeneous sensory inputs, generate and refine actions across multiple phases, and connect learned policies to reactive robot-end control. Building on this methodological view, we further examine the task settings and infrastructure requirements of physical interaction, thereby integrating both algorithmic and practical perspectives on force- and tactile-aware robot learning.

</details>


### [116] [Projection-Retraction MPPI: Exact Constraint-Manifold Control for Manipulators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07573)

Abstract: Model Predictive Path Integral (MPPI) control is widely used in manipulation for its gradient-free, parallel handling of non-convex costs. Manipulation tasks, however, often impose constraints that hold throughout the motion: a closed kinematic chain that two grasping arms keep exactly, or joint limits and obstacle clearances that are never crossed. MPPI handles such constraints only through the cost, as soft penalties that hold approximately and fail under a strong task cost. To address this, we propose Projection-Retraction MPPI (PR-MPPI), which enforces the constraints inside the sampled dynamics. At every rollout step, the sampled velocity is projected to satisfy both constraint types: the equality restricts it to a subspace, and each inequality to a half-space within that subspace, so inequality handling never breaks the equality. This projection, however, satisfies the constraints only to first order, and a finite step leaves a small drift off the equality. Therefore, we retract the returned command back onto the constraint to numerical tolerance and independent of task weighting. We validate PR-MPPI on 14-DoF dual-arm systems. In simulation, the returned commands satisfy the closed-chain equality to numerical tolerance through a joint-limit stress test and randomized obstacle avoidance. On real hardware, the arms of a Unitree H1-2 humanoid reactively avoid a moving obstacle. Code and experiment videos are available at https://rcilab.github.io/prmppi.

</details>

Abstract: Robotic construction systems often treat the material system and the robot as separate design problems, locating intelligence primarily in hardware, sensing, motion planning, and control. This project instead investigates how geometric intelligence can be encoded within architected material systems to simultaneously address requirements for robotic grasping, self-alignment, reversible connection, structural performance, and three-dimensional aggregation. We introduce a self-aligning compound nested lattice module composed of conjoined cuboctahedral-octahedral units. The cuboctahedral features of the modules provide defined surfaces for robotic grasping and alignment, while the octahedral features incorporate screw-releasable snap-fit connectors and corresponding receptors. Additionally, we present a nested arrangement that enables interlocking aggregation along the x, y, and z axes. We demonstrate the system through furniture and architectural scale structures assembled using both a robotic arm and mobile assembler. The resulting configurations include seating, spanning structures, surfaces, and vertical frames. Compression testing of the compound module produced a stiffness of 4,556 N/mm, a maximum load of 3,445 N, and a compressive modulus of 17.5 MPa. The modules can also be disassembled and reused across different configurations, supporting reconfigurable and circular construction.

</details>


### [118] [LIRA: Local Cross-Layer Information Routing for Vision-Language-Action Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07596)
*Zhewei Zhang,Puyue Wang,Guanren Qiao,Yijie Weng,Jiawei Hu,Guo Li,Lujia Wang,Junyan Wang,Tao Gu,Hongliang Lu,Guiliang Liu,Hong Jia,Xinhu Zheng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出LIRA，一种跨层动作条件机制，通过深度感知信息路由改进VLA模型中VLM到动作解码器的接口设计，提升机器人操作任务性能。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文聚焦于机器人操作领域的Vision-Language-Action (VLA)模型，与遥感卫星、Earth observation、目标检测、轻量化部署完全无关。属于泛机器人/具身智能方向，与本研究方向基本无关。

<details>

Model architecture: LIRA架构：基于预训练VLM，引入Parallel Fusion Block和LIRA Query特征，通过深度对齐的局部窗口进行跨层特征路由。骨干网络和动作解码器保持不变。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明任何轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）。模型配置为0.5B参数，但未涉及模型压缩或加速技术。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量（仅提及0.5B配置）、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等部署相关信息。且该模型面向地面机器人，非星上/边缘部署场景。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关，无任何可借鉴的遥感相关设计或部署优化策略。

Ideas for my research: 无直接可借鉴点。该论文属于机器人VLA领域，不涉及遥感图像、目标检测、模型轻量化或星上部署等核心问题。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models transform representations from pretrained vision-language models (VLMs) into robot actions, yet the interface that routes intermediate VLM features into action decoders remains underexplored. Existing designs either expose only a narrow part of the representation hierarchy or rigidly match each decoder block to one VLM layer, restricting access to complementary task evidence across depths. We introduce LIRA, a local cross-layer action-conditioning mechanism that formulates VLM-to-action conditioning as depth-aware information routing. LIRA operates on task-token features and LIRA Query features derived from intermediate VLM states, then assigns each Parallel Fusion Block a depth-aligned local window centered on its corresponding VLM layer. Parallel Fusion Blocks aggregate neighboring LIRA Query features and integrate them with task-token features and proprioceptive inputs before action prediction. This routing interface leaves the backbone architecture, action decoder, and supervised training recipe unchanged. Across LIBERO, LIBERO-Plus, CALVIN ABC$\rightarrow$D, and real-world manipulation, LIRA improves the principal aggregate metrics over the VLA-Adapter baseline under the same 0.5B-parameter configuration. In zero-shot transfer to LIBERO-Plus, LIRA increases average success from 59.1% to 78.0%, an 18.9-point gain indicating improved robustness under controlled distribution shifts.

</details>


### [119] [AdaDexGrasp: Adaptive Dexterous Grasping via 3D Visuo-Tactile Representation Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07600)

Abstract: Humans achieve stable and adaptive grasps by seamlessly integrating visual perception and tactile feedback, a capability that remains challenging to replicate in robotic systems. Existing robotic grasping approaches predominantly rely on visual inputs and lack mechanisms for tactile-guided adaptation after contact, limiting robustness and generalization. To address this challenge, we propose a unified visuo-tactile-fusion grasping framework that integrates grasp generation, feasibility prediction, and adaptive refinement. At its core, our method introduces an efficient visuo-tactile representation that tightly fuses object geometry with tactile feedback by associating tactile signals with finger identities. This unified representation supports contact-aware grasp pose generation during planning and tactile-guided refinement after contact, enabling the system to reason about fine-grained finger-object interactions and adjust grasps dynamically. Comprehensive experiments in both simulation and real-world environments demonstrate that our approach significantly enhances grasp success rates and generalization across diverse objects.

</details>


### [120] [Enhanced Real-Time 6-DOF Extended Reality Catheter Tracking for Evaluating Potential Improvement in Efficiency, Precision, and Depth Perception for Cardiac Interventions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07606)
*Mohsen Annabestani,Sandhya Sriram,Andrew Kuzemczak,S. Chiu Wong,Alexandros Sigaras,Bobak Mosadegh*

Abstract: Despite advances in 3D ultrasound, most percutaneous cardiac interventions still rely on 2D visualization, limiting depth perception and spatial understanding. To address this challenge, we developed an Extended Reality (XR)-based platform that enables real-time six-degree-of-freedom (6-DOF) catheter tracking and visualization within a patient-specific 3D heart model. The system combines a custom machine-vision algorithm for 5-DOF catheter tracking with a 3D-printed electromechanical encoder that measures catheter roll, providing complete 6-DOF motion reconstruction. In a proof-of-concept study, 20 novice medical students navigated an intracardiac echocardiography (ICE) catheter to six anatomical targets using either immersive 3D visualization or a conventional 2D cathlab-style view. Participants in the 3D condition completed the task in 54.6 seconds and traveled 1,939 mm on average, compared with 267.5 seconds and 7,854 mm in the 2D condition. Therefore, the XR-based 3D system was more than 5x faster and required ~5x less catheter travel. The 3D mode also improved targeting precision and reduced performance variability. Participants consistently rated immersive visualization higher for accuracy, speed, usability, and clinical value. Kinematic analysis showed smoother depth-axis navigation in 3D, whereas 2D users relied on repeated corrective movements. These findings demonstrate that XR-based visualization can substantially improve procedural training efficiency, precision, and motor control.

</details>


### [121] [GWM-VLA: Geometry-Aware Latent World Modeling for Vision-Language-Action Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07619)
*Yanping Zhao,Hang Yu,Yiwei Wang,Chen Ye,Siyu Tian,Di Zhang,Qingjun Wang,Qian Chen,Junqiao Zhao,Chen Ye,Guang Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出GWM-VLA，一种几何感知的潜在世界模型框架，用于提升机器人操作任务在视觉和环境变化下的鲁棒性。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文聚焦于机器人操作（robotic manipulation）中的VLA模型鲁棒性问题，与遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱/多光谱、空间在轨智能等方向完全无关。不涉及目标检测、小目标检测、旋转框检测、遥感场景理解等任务。也不涉及轻量化、剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS、实时推理、边缘/星上部署等任何模型压缩或加速技术。属于泛机器人/具身智能领域，与本研究方向基本无关。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及模型轻量化方向基本无关，不建议投入时间精读。

  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：机器人操作（robotic manipulation）；应用场景：仿真和真实世界的机器人抓取与操作；传感器类型：多视角相机（multi-view observations，包括wrist view腕部视角）；目标类别：机器人操作中的物体（未具体说明类别）。

Model architecture: GWM-VLA框架，包含三个核心组件：(1) 几何感知多视角状态编码（geometry-aware multi-view state encoding），使用VGGT-Ω联合聚合多视角观测；(2) 全局上下文条件的目标视角预测（global context-conditioned target-view prediction），潜在世界模型预测选定目标视角的下一时刻patch tokens；(3) 共享潜在-动作表征（shared latent-action representations），由机器人动作监督引导。使用flow-matching action head生成动作。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明任何轻量化方法。不涉及剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络设计等。

Experiments: 摘要仅提及在仿真和真实世界环境中验证了GWM-VLA的有效性和鲁棒性，未提供具体数值结果、对比方法或消融实验细节。

Limitations: (1) 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测完全无关，无法直接借鉴；(2) 摘要未说明模型参数量、计算量、推理速度等关键效率指标；(3) 未讨论在资源受限设备上的部署可行性；(4) 潜在世界模型预测patch tokens的计算开销可能较大，不适合星上实时推理。

Ideas for my research: (1) 多视角几何感知编码的思路：如果遥感任务涉及多视角/多时相卫星图像融合，可借鉴其几何感知的多视角状态编码思想；(2) 潜在世界模型预测未来帧的思路：在遥感视频目标检测中，可考虑利用时序预测辅助检测；(3) 共享潜在表征的思路：在遥感多任务学习（如检测+分割）中可借鉴共享表征设计。但整体而言，该论文与"在轨遥感目标检测模型轻量化"课题的直接可借鉴点非常有限。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models achieve strong robotic manipulation performance but often degrade under visual and environmental shifts. Latent world modeling offers a promising approach to improving robustness, yet existing methods commonly encode camera views independently and predict holistic scene dynamics without explicitly modeling their geometric relationships. We propose GWM-VLA, a geometry-aware latent world modeling framework for VLA learning. GWM-VLA combines geometry-aware multi-view state encoding, global context-conditioned target-view prediction, and shared latent-action representations grounded by robot-action supervision. Specifically, VGGT-$Ω$ jointly aggregates multi-view observations at each timestep to construct geometry-aware multi-view states. The latent world model predicts the next-step patch tokens of a selected target view using patch and register tokens obtained after multi-view aggregation, thereby retaining multi-view geometric information without predicting the complete multi-view state. We use the wrist view as the target in our experiments, placing greater emphasis on end-effector motion and local gripper-object interactions. Finally, the shared latent-action representations condition both the latent world model and the flow-matching action head, allowing latent-prediction supervision and ground-truth robot-action supervision to jointly shape the same latent-action representations. Experiments across both simulation and real-world environments demonstrate the effectiveness and robustness of GWM-VLA.

</details>

Abstract: Tracked robots are widely used in unstructured environments; however, their obstacle traversal capability is fundamentally limited by a tradeoff between front-end reachability and locomotion stability. This study presents TRASER (Tracked Robot with Articulated Spine for Extended Reach), a reconfigurable tracked robot capable of relocating both its articulation point and internal mass. TRASER employs a tape-spring mechanism that localizes compliance to the bending region while maintaining high stiffness in the remaining body, thereby improving both front-end reachability and center-of-mass (CoM) shifting capability. Geometric and static models are developed to analyze the effects of articulation point and CoM position on step and ditch traversal performances. Experiments demonstrate step traversal, suspended-platform traversal, and ditch traversal of 74\%, 66\%, and 59\% of the robot body length, respectively. To the best of our knowledge, these results represent the highest reported obstacle traversal capabilities among tracked mobile robots.

</details>
